- **Clear private-surface accounting (`.planning/phases/06.48-link-access-check-as-a-private-function-narrow-obj-in-cv-has/06.48-04-PLAN.md:114-141`):** Documents the exact inventory of removed and retained private adapters/seams in accordance with the Developer Maintainability rule and Issue #407's acceptance criteria.

### 3. Concerns
- **LOW — `test_query_plain_child_link_cost` direct `cv_has_access(current, parent)` call (`.planning/phases/06.48-link-access-check-as-a-private-function-narrow-obj-in-cv-has/06.48-04-PLAN.md:159-175` and `tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:1100`):**
  - *Input:* `tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:1099-1100` in `test_query_plain_child_link_cost` calls `assert target.cv_has_access(current, parent) is True` in the `layer == "own_access"` baseline loop, and `tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:335, 407` call `PlainList.cv_has_access(actor, parent)` / `family.list.cv_has_access(actor, parent_obj)`.
  - *What happens:* Those calls still pass `parent` to `cv_has_access` on a list view in the test file (they do not fail because `CrudView.cv_has_access` and `CrudViewPermissionRequiredMixin.cv_has_access` ignore `obj`).
  - *What should happen:* While not causing a test failure, passing `None` at lines 335, 407, and 1100 of `tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py` aligns the test's own `own_access` baseline call with the narrowed `cv_has_access(user, None)` contract that `rendered_data` now invokes.

### 4. Suggestions

### 5. Risk Assessment
**LOW** — The two parent-in-hand sites follow the exact pattern proven in Plan 02 on the navbar child item, and all 13 test adjustments in `test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py` are pre-identified.

## 06.48-05

### 1. Summary
`.planning/phases/06.48-link-access-check-as-a-private-function-narrow-obj-in-cv-has/06.48-05-PLAN.md` adds `_page_action_object` in `src/crud_views/lib/link_access.py` and the private classmethod seam `_cv_page_link_ancestors` on `CrudView` (returning `None`) and `GuardianCreateViewPermissionRequired` (returning a tuple when nested). It updates `ContextButton.get_context` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/buttons.py:95-129`) and the view-key branch of `CrudView._cv_get_action_context` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:898-921`) so that a nested Guardian create goes through `_link_access` while all other targets keep their existing code path with narrowed `obj` and `_page_action_object` (D-31).

### 2. Strengths
- **Clean realization of D-31, D-27, and D-33 without leaking Guardian names into core (`.planning/phases/06.48-link-access-check-as-a-private-function-narrow-obj-in-cv-has/06.48-05-PLAN.md:148-173, 214-237`):** `_cv_page_link_ancestors` returns `None` on `CrudView` and a tuple (`(resolved_parent,)` or `()`) on a nested `GuardianCreateViewPermissionRequired`. Core code never checks `hasattr(..., "cv_create_has_access")`, and the four D-27/D-33 denials (child list filter, `ManyToMany` child page, create page itself, and sibling button) all fall out of returning `()` when `page_obj` is not a single-FK own-model instance.
- **Preserves unguarded view behavior for core targets (`.planning/phases/06.48-link-access-check-as-a-private-function-narrow-obj-in-cv-has/06.48-05-PLAN.md:181-183, 207-210`):** Because core nested creates return `None` from `_cv_page_link_ancestors`, they still pass through `CreateView._cv_action_enabled_for_rendering` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/create.py:52-75`), keeping all unguarded `RequestFactory` tests in `tests/test1/test_core_nested_create_state.py` green without edits.
- **Eliminates redundant work on Guardian child list pages:** When `GuardianQuerysetMixin.cv_get_context` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:326`) calls `super().cv_get_context("create", obj=None, ...)`, `CrudView._cv_get_action_context` now gets `()` from `_cv_page_link_ancestors(self, None)` and short-circuits via D-16 without calling `_cv_action_enabled_for_rendering` or `cv_get_parent_object()`, before `GuardianQuerysetMixin.cv_get_context` stamps the real result with `known = (_resolved_parent(self),)`.

### 3. Concerns
- **LOW — Passing a parent model instance as `obj` to `cv_get_context("create", obj=parent)` on a Guardian child view in tests:**
  - *Input:* A test calling `child_view.cv_get_context("create", obj=parent_instance, user=user)` directly on a Guardian child view (as `tests/test1/test_core_nested_create_state.py:186` does for a core child view).
  - *What happens:* Under `GuardianCreateViewPermissionRequired._cv_page_link_ancestors` (`.planning/phases/06.48-link-access-check-as-a-private-function-narrow-obj-in-cv-has/06.48-05-PLAN.md:221-223`), `isinstance(page_obj, cls.cv_viewset.model)` is `False` when `page_obj` is a parent instance, so it returns `()` and denies. Plan 08 (`.planning/phases/06.48-link-access-check-as-a-private-function-narrow-obj-in-cv-has/06.48-08-PLAN.md:122`, fact F8) explicitly documents this ("A parent instance passed as `obj` to `cv_get_context` on a child page no longer stands for the parent"), which is consistent and intentional.

### 4. Suggestions
- In `GuardianCreateViewPermissionRequired._cv_page_link_ancestors` (`.planning/phases/06.48-link-access-check-as-a-private-function-narrow-obj-in-cv-has/06.48-05-PLAN.md:220-225`), include the inline comment explaining why `page_obj` must be an instance of `cls.cv_viewset.model` (distinguishing a child object page from the create page itself, a sibling button, or the filter on a child list page).

### 5. Risk Assessment
**LOW** — The split between `_cv_page_link_ancestors` returning `None` (core/non-create targets) vs a tuple (nested Guardian create) isolates the behavioral change to the exact targets specified by D-31.

## 06.48-06

### 1. Summary
`.planning/phases/06.48-link-access-check-as-a-private-function-narrow-obj-in-cv-has/06.48-06-PLAN.md` applies the `_cv_page_link_ancestors` and `_page_action_object` rule from Plan 05 to the remaining four sibling-page sites: `ParentContextButton.get_context` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/buttons.py:145-200`), `SiblingContextButton.get_context` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/buttons.py:277-311`), the non-child branch of `CardListView.cv_get_card_action_data` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/card.py:76-91`), and the `cv_context_has_permission` template filter (`src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:649-655`). It also adds `test_action_object_same_for_link_and_request` to prove AC-4 (D-13).

### 2. Strengths
- **Unified mechanism across all six sibling-page sites (`.planning/phases/06.48-link-access-check-as-a-private-function-narrow-obj-in-cv-has/06.48-06-PLAN.md:130-143`):** All six sites use the exact same two-step pattern (`_cv_page_link_ancestors` branch for nested Guardian create, `_page_action_object` + narrowed `obj if cls.cv_object else None` for everything else).
- **Direct end-to-end proof of D-13 (`.planning/phases/06.48-link-access-check-as-a-private-function-narrow-obj-in-cv-has/06.48-06-PLAN.md:283-294`):** `test_action_object_same_for_link_and_request` compares the object received by `cv_action_enabled` during link rendering against the object received during a direct request for an object view, a nested list (`home` on a nested detail page), and a nested create, in both core and Guardian compositions.

### 3. Concerns
- **HIGH — `ParentContextButton` targeting parent `"create"` on a grandchild `GuardianListViewPermissionRequired` page is overwritten by `GuardianQuerysetMixin.cv_get_context` (`.planning/phases/06.48-link-access-check-as-a-private-function-narrow-obj-in-cv-has/06.48-06-PLAN.md:194-213` and `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:328-357`):**
  - *Input:* `test_parent_button_reaches_nested_guardian_create_through_the_check` (`.planning/phases/06.48-link-access-check-as-a-private-function-narrow-obj-in-cv-has/06.48-06-PLAN.md:194-197`) places a `ParentContextButton(key="...", key_target="create")` on a grandchild page in the three-level Guardian chain (`helpers.pub -> helpers.book -> helpers.note` from `tests/test1/test_ancestor_helpers.py:61-68`) where the target is `helpers.book.Create` (one level up). Suppose the grandchild page rendered is `helpers.note.List` (`GuardianListViewPermissionRequired`, where `obj=None` and `helpers.note.vs` also has `helpers.note.Create` registered at `tests/test1/test_ancestor_helpers.py:56`).
  - *What happens:*
    1. During `helpers.note.List.cv_get_context(key="parent_create", obj=None, user=user)`, `super().cv_get_context` invokes `ParentContextButton.get_context`. That method looks up `parent_obj = data.book`, calls `helpers.book.Create._cv_page_link_ancestors(context.view, data.book)` which returns `(data.penguin,)`, and runs `_link_access(helpers.book.Create, user, None, (data.penguin,))` (1st parent gate, on `helpers.pub` for `data.penguin`).
    2. Control then returns to `GuardianQuerysetMixin.cv_get_context` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:328-342`). Because `obj is None and key is not None and self.cv_viewset.has_parent` is `True`, line 332 fetches `context_button = self.cv_get_context_button(key)` (the `ParentContextButton`), line 333 computes `target_key = context_button.key_target ...` which is `"create"`, and line 335 resolves `target_cls = self.cv_viewset.get_view_class("create")` which is `helpers.note.Create` (the *grandchild* create view, not `helpers.book.Create`!).
    3. `GuardianQuerysetMixin.cv_get_context` then calls `_link_access(helpers.note.Create, user, None, (data.book,), rendering_view=self)` (2nd parent gate, on `helpers.book` for `data.book`) and overwrites `ctx["cv_access"]` and `ctx["cv_action_enabled"]` with the wrong view's result!
    4. Even if `test_parent_button_reaches_nested_guardian_create_through_the_check` happens to use `helpers.note.Detail` (`obj=note`) instead of `helpers.note.List` (`obj=None`), any real `ParentContextButton(key_target="create")` on a grandchild `GuardianListViewPermissionRequired` page (or any `SiblingContextButton(key="create", sibling_key="create")` on a child `GuardianListViewPermissionRequired` page) will be corrupted by `GuardianQuerysetMixin.cv_get_context`.
  - *What should happen:* In `GuardianQuerysetMixin.cv_get_context` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:332-335`), only treat `context_button` as targeting `self.cv_viewset` when `context_button is None or type(context_button).get_context is ContextButton.get_context`. When `context_button` is a `ParentContextButton`, `ChildContextButton`, or `SiblingContextButton`, skip the child-list create override in `GuardianQuerysetMixin.cv_get_context`. Also test `ParentContextButton(key_target="create")` on a grandchild Guardian *list* page in `test_parent_button_reaches_nested_guardian_create_through_the_check`.

### 4. Suggestions
- Fix `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:332-335` (either in Plan 02 when `GuardianQuerysetMixin.cv_get_context` is edited, or in Plan 06 Task `06.48-06-01`) so `GuardianQuerysetMixin.cv_get_context` ignores `ContextButton` subclasses that override `get_context`.
- In `test_parent_button_reaches_nested_guardian_create_through_the_check` (`.planning/phases/06.48-link-access-check-as-a-private-function-narrow-obj-in-cv-has/06.48-06-PLAN.md:194-197`), exercise `ParentContextButton` from `helpers.note.List` (or both `List` and `Detail`) so the interaction with `GuardianQuerysetMixin.cv_get_context` is covered.

### 5. Risk Assessment
**MEDIUM** — The interaction between `ParentContextButton`/`SiblingContextButton` and `GuardianQuerysetMixin.cv_get_context` on list pages (`obj=None`) is a real defect if not guarded; once guarded, risk is low.

## 06.48-07

### 1. Summary
`.planning/phases/06.48-link-access-check-as-a-private-function-narrow-obj-in-cv-has/06.48-07-PLAN.md` narrows `GuardianCreateViewPermissionRequired.cv_has_access` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:347-386`) so that a nested create returns `False` for every `obj` argument (Option G1, D-25), rewrites the listed direct-call assertions in `tests/test1/test_ancestor_helpers.py` and the four Guardian test suites to call `cv_create_has_access(user, None, obj)` while asserting `cv_has_access` is `False`, updates the public docstrings on `CrudView` and `GuardianCreateViewPermissionRequired`, and adds `test_no_package_call_site_passes_a_parent_to_cv_has_access`.

### 2. Strengths
- **Complete and verified D-25 line inventory (`.planning/phases/06.48-link-access-check-as-a-private-function-narrow-obj-in-cv-has/06.48-07-PLAN.md:107-125`):** Every single line of `tests/test1/test_ancestor_helpers.py` that calls `cv_has_access` on a nested Guardian create (`198-201, 227, 228, 239, 248, 260, 337, 351, 354`) is accounted for, and every request-gate assertion in the 18 `test_ancestor_*.py` files remains untouched.
- **Fails closed in the safe direction (`.planning/phases/06.48-link-access-check-as-a-private-function-narrow-obj-in-cv-has/06.48-07-PLAN.md:94-99, 165-167`):** Returning `False` for all `obj` when `cls.cv_viewset.parent is not None` guarantees that no direct caller (template filter, custom code, or `SiblingContextButton`) can ever receive `"allowed"` from `cv_has_access` on a nested Guardian create without a parent check.
- **Accurate docstring contract on `CrudView.cv_action_enabled` (`.planning/phases/06.48-link-access-check-as-a-private-function-narrow-obj-in-cv-has/06.48-07-PLAN.md:146-158, 203-206`):** Explicitly corrects the pre-existing overclaim in `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:545` ("evaluated only AFTER `cv_has_access` has passed") to state which controls run state after permission in stage 1 and which still evaluate state independently.

### 3. Concerns
- **MEDIUM — Wrapping the parent ViewSet's target views in `test_no_package_call_site_passes_a_parent_to_cv_has_access` (`.planning/phases/06.48-link-access-check-as-a-private-function-narrow-obj-in-cv-has/06.48-07-PLAN.md:191-199`):**
  - *Input:* `test_no_package_call_site_passes_a_parent_to_cv_has_access` wraps `cv_has_access` on "the list, detail, update and create classes of a core nested ViewSet and of a Guardian nested ViewSet" and renders every package control including "a parent button".
  - *What happens:* When a `ParentContextButton` is rendered on a child page (e.g., `BookListView` or `BookDetailView`), its target view class `cls` is a view of the *parent* ViewSet (`PublisherListView` or `PublisherDetailView`), not of the child `Book` ViewSet (`src/crud_views/lib/view/buttons.py:152-154`). If the test only wraps `cv_has_access` on the child ViewSet's classes, rendering `ParentContextButton` on the child page will call `PublisherListView.cv_has_access` / `PublisherDetailView.cv_has_access` unwrapped, never recording the call!
  - *What should happen:* Either also wrap `cv_has_access` on the parent ViewSet's `list`, `detail`, `update`, and `create` classes (for both core and Guardian parent ViewSets), or render `ParentContextButton` from a grandchild ViewSet page whose parent is the wrapped child ViewSet, and assert that at least one call from each listed control was recorded.
- **LOW — `test_child_page_inactive_actor_denied` in `tests/test1/test_guardian_parent_none.py:923` (`.planning/phases/06.48-link-access-check-as-a-private-function-narrow-obj-in-cv-has/06.48-07-PLAN.md:136`):**
  - *Input:* `tests/test1/test_guardian_parent_none.py:923` asserts `assert pn.BookCreate.cv_has_access(user, child) is False` for an inactive actor `user`.
  - *What happens:* Because `pn.BookCreate.cv_has_access` now returns `False` unconditionally for every actor and every `obj`, line 923 passes trivially without testing the inactive-actor denial on the create helper.
  - *What should happen:* Add `assert pn.BookCreate.cv_create_has_access(user, None, child) is False` next to line 923 in `tests/test1/test_guardian_parent_none.py` (just as Task `06.48-07-01` does for `test_inactive_access_helpers_deny` in `tests/test1/test_guardian_inactive.py:458-466`).

### 4. Suggestions
- In `test_no_package_call_site_passes_a_parent_to_cv_has_access` (`.planning/phases/06.48-link-access-check-as-a-private-function-narrow-obj-in-cv-has/06.48-07-PLAN.md:191-199`), wrap `cv_has_access` on the parent ViewSet's view classes (and sibling ViewSet's view classes for `SiblingContextButton`) in addition to the child ViewSet's view classes, so every control's target is actually instrumented.
- In `tests/test1/test_guardian_parent_none.py:923`, add `assert pn.BookCreate.cv_create_has_access(user, None, child) is False` alongside `assert pn.BookCreate.cv_has_access(user, child) is False`.

### 5. Risk Assessment
**LOW** — All package callers have already been migrated off cases 3 and 4 in Plans 02–06, so narrowing `GuardianCreateViewPermissionRequired.cv_has_access` cannot affect any rendered control in the characterization matrix.

## Cross-Plan Summary & Overall Risk Assessment

- **Overall Risk Level:** **LOW-to-MEDIUM**
- **Assessment:** The 9-plan sequence is exceptionally well-grounded in the codebase (`dbf1e6c4`) and faithful to the approved concept (`superpowers/specs/2026-11-23-link-access-check-design.md`, including section 12.6 D-25–D-33 and the Developer Maintainability rule). Wave ordering is sound: Wave 1 freezes the characterization matrix and costs before any production edit; Wave 2 builds the check and proves it on two tracer sites; Wave 3 unit-tests the check; Waves 4–6 migrate parent-in-hand and sibling-page sites while keeping `GuardianCreateViewPermissionRequired.cv_has_access` tolerant until Wave 7; Wave 7 narrows `cv_has_access` to fail closed and rewrites the direct-call tests; Waves 8–9 update the docs and verify the final gate.
- **Top Actionable Fixes Before/During Execution:**
  1. **`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:332-337` (`06.48-02` / `06.48-06`, HIGH):** Guard `GuardianQuerysetMixin.cv_get_context` so `context_button` only triggers the child-list create override when `context_button is None or type(context_button).get_context is ContextButton.get_context`. Otherwise, a `ParentContextButton(key_target="create")` on a grandchild Guardian list page (`obj=None`) or a `SiblingContextButton(key="create", sibling_key="create")` on a child Guardian list page will have its `ctx` overwritten with the current ViewSet's create view.
  2. **`docs/faq.md:142-159` (`06.48-09`, MEDIUM):** Rewrite `docs/faq.md:142-159` against F1, F6, F8, and F9 (mirroring `docs/reference/context_buttons.md:596-618`), as those lines currently state that the filter resolves the child object's FK parent, returns `False` on a list page because there is no object, and that "the helpers" accept an explicit parent instance.
  3. **`tests/test1/test_link_access.py::test_no_package_call_site_passes_a_parent_to_cv_has_access` (`06.48-07`, MEDIUM):** Ensure the `cv_has_access` recording wrapper also wraps the parent ViewSet's and sibling ViewSet's view classes (or renders `ParentContextButton` from a grandchild page) so `ParentContextButton` and `SiblingContextButton` calls to `cv_has_access` are actually captured.
  4. **`tests/test1/test_link_access_matrix.py` (`06.48-01`, MEDIUM):** Ensure `inactive` actor requests in Task `06.48-01-02` use `INACTIVE_BACKENDS` / `ALLOW_INACTIVE` (`tests/test1/test_authorization_matrix.py:31-32`) so `Client.force_login` preserves the authenticated-inactive user on `request.user`.


---

## Consensus Summary

Cycle 1. Three reviewers produced a review with `file:line` evidence, and each covered all nine plans. None of the
three sections opens with an evidence-quality discount marker, so all three count at full weight. grok-review and
gemini-review share the OpenCode adapter: where they agree, the agreement is cross-model, not cross-tool.

No reviewer disputes a locked decision. astra-review states that it did not treat the farther-ancestor case (D-26)
or the queryset-only case (D-30) as a defect. gemini-review calls its one finding on the object a parent instance
stands for (plan 05) "consistent and intentional".

Counts for this cycle: one HIGH concern, 24 actionable non-HIGH concerns. The ledger below lists each one.

### Agreed Strengths

- **Order of the waves.** The baseline is frozen before any production edit, and `cv_has_access` on a nested
  Guardian create is narrowed (plan 07) only after every package caller has moved off the parent cases (plans 02
  to 06). All three reviewers name this as the main safety property.
- **The per-target seam.** `_cv_link_permission` keeps three existing, different child-link rules in the classes
  that own them today (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:528-541`, `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:297-305`,
  `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:387-390`). Core names no Guardian hook. (astra, grok, gemini)
- **Gate modules stay untouched.** `ancestors.py` and `ancestor_db.py` are byte-identical, so D-14 is a diff check.
  Each site keeps today's `view=` value for the provider call, so checker reuse and trace ownership do not move
  (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/ancestors.py:61-72`). (grok, gemini; astra for the `view=` value)
- **The D-25 line tables are exact.** The four lines of plan 04 and the lines of plan 07 match
  `tests/test1/test_ancestor_helpers.py`. (astra, grok, gemini)
- **The one changed cost is named and tied to evidence** (`test_query_denied_actors_and_custom_do_no_permission_work`,
  `test_reached_policy_errors_propagate`), not to a guessed number. (astra, grok, gemini)
- **`None` versus tuple in `_cv_page_link_ancestors`.** "Keep your code path" and "the check decides, and may deny"
  are told apart without a keyword on the check. `_resolved_ancestor` reads guard state and runs no query
  (`src/crud_views/lib/ancestors.py:576-585`). (astra, grok, gemini)

### Agreed Concerns

Raised by two or more reviewers:

1. **The recording test of plan 07 does not observe every target it renders** (astra LOW, gemini MEDIUM; ledger
   A-14). `test_no_package_call_site_passes_a_parent_to_cv_has_access` wraps `cv_has_access` on the list, detail,
   update and create classes of the nested ViewSets (`06.48-07-PLAN.md:233`). It also renders delete actions, a
   parent button and a sibling button. The delete class is not wrapped (astra). The parent button's target is a
   class of the parent ViewSet and the sibling button's target a class of the sibling ViewSet
   (`src/crud_views/lib/view/buttons.py:152-154`), and neither is wrapped (gemini). An unobserved call cannot
   fail the assertion.
2. **The shared spy loop of `test_navbar_isolated_costs`** (grok MEDIUM, gemini suggestion; ledger A-09). The loop
   asserts `spy.call_args.args[1] is resolved_owner` for every spy (`tests/test1/test_navbar_costs.py:150-152`).
   For `_cv_link_permission` the owner is the third positional argument and the second is `None`. Plan 02 names
   the new expected calls but not this identity assertion.

Raised by more than one reviewer as a theme, each with a different case:

3. **The D-18 actor rule is thinly pinned.** astra: the nested Guardian create seam answers with
   `cv_create_has_access` only, which does not apply the signed-in-and-active rule to an anonymous actor under a
   protected core parent (A-07, A-08). grok: no test catches an actor rule placed before the "no higher level"
   return of `_higher_ancestor_gates` (A-10). gemini: that function denies an anonymous actor before it validates
   the policy, where the nearest level validates first (A-11).
4. **The matrix of plan 01 can pass without proving its contract.** astra: case ids collide and two promised
   scenarios have no capture instruction (A-01, A-02). grok: the verify threshold is 300 for 504 cells (A-03).
   gemini: an inactive actor logged in through the test client becomes anonymous without the inactive backends
   (A-04).
5. **Documentation edits miss passages.** gemini: `docs/faq.md:142-159` (A-23). grok: the queryset-only override
   case (A-19). gemini: `docs/reference/card-list-view.md:308-309` (A-20).

### Divergent Views

- **Severity of the list-page overwrite.** Only gemini-review raises it, as HIGH (ledger H-01). astra-review and
  grok-review read the same branch of `GuardianQuerysetMixin.cv_get_context` and report no defect there; grok
  lists the plan-02 rewrite of that branch as a strength of plan 05. The mechanism is confirmed in source (see
  H-01). It exists on the unchanged tree, so no rendered result changes in stage 1. What it breaks is a truth and
  a test of plan 06.
- **Risk per plan.** astra rates plans 04 and 06 to 09 LOW. grok rates plans 01, 02 and 04 to 08 MEDIUM, mostly
  for documentation residue. gemini rates plans 01, 03 to 05 and 07 to 09 LOW and plans 02 and 06 MEDIUM.
- **The seam of the nested Guardian create.** astra sees a D-18 gap in it (A-07). grok and gemini see it as
  correct and name the single parent gate as a strength.
- **Private surface.** grok notes that two seams, five module functions and two kept adapters are more than the
  concept's "one function", and that this is the smallest shape that keeps Guardian rules out of core under D-31.
  No reviewer asks for a change.

### Concern ledger — cycle 1

Severity is the reviewer's. "Checked" says what the orchestrator confirmed in source or in the plan text.

**HIGH**

| ID | Reviewer | Plan | Concern | Checked |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| H-01 | gemini | 06 (also 02, 01) | `GuardianQuerysetMixin.cv_get_context` resolves the `key_target` of any context button against the page's own ViewSet (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:328-342`). Input: a `ParentContextButton(key_target="create")` on a grandchild Guardian list page whose own ViewSet registers `create`. What happens: the button's result is computed for the parent ViewSet's create, then overwritten with the result for the page's own create, with a second parent gate. What should happen: the override applies only to a plain `ContextButton` (or to no button). Plan 02 keeps "the existing condition that selects a nested Guardian create target" (`06.48-02-PLAN.md:268`). Plan 06 states "exactly one parent gate" and "the answer comes from `cv_create_has_access(user, None, parent)`" for a parent button (`06.48-06-PLAN.md:27`), and its test names "a grandchild page" without saying which (`06.48-06-PLAN.md:198`). | Confirmed. `ParentContextButton` and `SiblingContextButton` subclass `ContextButton` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/buttons.py:132`, `:255`); `key_target` is a base field (`:35`); the `helpers` fixture registers `Create` on the grandchild ViewSet (`tests/test1/test_ancestor_helpers.py:49-58`, `:100`). The overwrite exists on the unchanged tree. Judgment-class, one reviewer, no discount marker: it counts. |

Two cells get one key. | Add the target or variant to the case id and assert that the generated ids are unique. |

| A-04 | gemini | MEDIUM | 01 | Page-level cells and the navbar cells use the test client. `force_login` of an inactive user leaves `request.user` anonymous unless `INACTIVE_BACKENDS` is set and the backend is passed (`tests/test1/test_authorization_matrix.py:31-32`, `tests/test1/test_guardian_parent_none.py:703-707`). The plan lists these names under `read_first` but gives no instruction. | State the settings override and `force_login(actor, backend=ALLOW_INACTIVE)` for the `inactive` actor. |
| A-05 | grok | LOW | 01 | No Resource leaf in the matrix. Research pitfall 9 says to add one if a child link can target one (`06.48-RESEARCH.md:781-785`). | Add the cell, or record why no child link can target a Resource leaf. |

## Cross-artifact fact drift

Advisory only. Nothing in this section counts toward `current_high` or `current_actionable`.

**Phase status (deterministic seam).** `gsd_run drift-guard phase-status --phase 06.48` returned
`{"verdict":"uncheckable","reason":"phase_not_in_roadmap","stateStatus":"Ready to execute","roadmapStatus":null,"authority":"STATE.md"}`.
The verdict is recorded as uncheckable and is not read as consistent. `.planning/ROADMAP.md:77` and `:2383` do
carry the phase; the seam did not find a status for it. That is a limit of the seam, not a finding.

**Success criteria and must-have truths (ROADMAP.md is the authority).** The roadmap section of the phase
(`.planning/ROADMAP.md:2383-2421`) has no "Success Criteria" list. Its goal (`:2385`) and its cross-cutting
constraint (`:2420-2421`) were compared with the `must_haves.truths` of all nine plans.

- Constraint "every cell of the characterization matrix, every gate event of `test_ancestor_costs.py` and every query
  total of `test_navbar_costs.py` is unchanged; the golden files are byte-identical": stated the same way in
  `06.48-02-PLAN.md:38-39`, `06.48-05-PLAN.md:34`, `06.48-06-PLAN.md:31` and `06.48-09-PLAN.md:23`.
  `06.48-04-PLAN.md:32` adds one named cost outside those suites. Plans may add truths. No contradiction.
- Goal "one private link check (parent gates, own permission, state)": `06.48-02-PLAN.md:27`. The limit to a nested
  Guardian create at the sibling-page sites (`06.48-05-PLAN.md:25`) follows D-31, which the roadmap's source line
  (`:2386`) names. A difference in level of detail, not a contradiction.
- Plan list and waves (`.planning/ROADMAP.md:2393-2418`): nine plans, waves 1 to 9, matching the `wave` and
  `depends_on` frontmatter of the nine plans. No contradiction.

**Requirement IDs (ROADMAP.md is the authority).** `.planning/ROADMAP.md:2387` says "None assigned; use the issue
acceptance criteria". All nine plans carry `requirements: []` and say so in their objective. No contradiction.

**Decision terms (CONTEXT.md is the authority).** The decision numbers the plans use (D-04 to D-07, D-10 to D-20,
D-25 to D-33) were compared with their definitions in `06.48-CONTEXT.md:41-124`.

One finding:

- **FD-1 (advisory): the object `cv_action_enabled` receives for a nested list link.**
  - `06.48-CONTEXT.md:56-58` (D-13): "The object passed to `cv_action_enabled` for a link follows the request's
    `cv_get_action_object`: the target's own object for an object view, the immediate parent for any nested view
    without an own object (list and create alike), otherwise nothing."
  - `06.48-07-PLAN.md:175`: "One exception: on a view that did not run the ancestor guard, a nested list receives
    `None`." The same exception is in `06.48-05-PLAN.md:28-29` and `:196-199` and in fact F2 of
    `06.48-08-PLAN.md:125`.
  - Divergence: D-13 names no exception; the plans state one. The plans derive it from D-04 and D-27 (no lookup to
    decide a link; the parent is the one the request resolved), and they publish it in the migration note. The
    concept document does not record it either (section 12.6 was searched).
  - Authority: CONTEXT.md. To collapse the two copies, either record the exception next to D-13 (and in concept
    12.6) if the maintainer accepts it, or change the plans so that D-13 holds without exception.

Compared and not flagged:

- D-16 and the consequence line under D-27 (`06.48-CONTEXT.md:145-147`, "a view without a resolved parent ... is
  denied under D-16") against `06.48-05-PLAN.md:29` ("a core control does not disappear on a view that resolved no
  parent"). Read with D-31 (`06.48-CONTEXT.md:106-112`), the denial applies to a target that goes through the
  check, which at these sites is the nested Guardian create only. The consequence block also says it holds no
  decisions. The two agree.
- D-25 (`06.48-CONTEXT.md:78-84`) names `tests/test1/test_ancestor_resolution.py` as a file with link-helper
  assertions; `06.48-07-PLAN.md:141-142` rewrites no line there. The only link helper that file calls is
  `_cv_action_enabled_for_rendering` (lines 749 and 771), which stage 1 does not change. Nothing to rewrite is not a
  contradiction.
- D-28 ("is shown today"), D-32 and D-33 against `06.48-05-PLAN.md:30-32`, `06.48-06-PLAN.md:27-28` and facts F7
  and F9 of `06.48-08-PLAN.md:130-132`: the same statements.
- D-26 and D-30 against T-06.48-14 (`06.48-06-PLAN.md:324`) and fact F10 (`06.48-08-PLAN.md:133`): the same
  statements.
- The three private adapters named under "Claude's Discretion" (`06.48-CONTEXT.md:221-222`) are non-authoritative
  by design and were not compared.

## Verification coverage

- **Authority.** Effective authority `intel`; its index is empty and regex/JS-only. Every Python class, method,
  function, field, fixture and test name the plans cite as existing is UNCHECKABLE at that authority. A direct
  source occurrence was found for each (third column of the symbol table). No signature is reported as verified;
  signatures cannot be asserted under `grep` or `intel`.
- **Paths.** Every cited path was resolved on the filesystem. 78 resolve. 15 are artifacts this phase creates and
  were excluded (they do not exist yet, as expected). Four path-like tokens are not paths (a branch name, two
  case-id format strings, one prose phrase). No path is MISSING.
- **CLI flags.** 14 flags of `git`, `pytest`, `grep`, `test`, `mkdocs` and `ruff` are UNCHECKABLE: `intel` has no
  CLI index and this pass ran none of the plans' commands. The `[VALIDATED: bash -n exit 0]` stamps in the plans
  are syntax checks by the planner and were not repeated.
- **External symbols.** 21 Django, pytest, `unittest.mock` and stdlib names are UNCHECKABLE (outside the project).
- **Excluded.** 68 symbols are declared under "Artifacts this phase produces" (new module, new seams, new tests,
  their parameters) and were not verified, by rule.
- **Not symbols.** Plan frontmatter keys, plan element names, verification stamps and `pytest -k` fragments were
  listed and not verified.
- **Line ranges in `read_first`.** The plans cite many line ranges as reading hints (for example
  `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py (lines 523-575)`). The files were verified; the ranges were spot-checked where
  a plan's argument depends on them (listed under "Quoted source lines") and otherwise not checked one by one.
- **Fact drift.** Phase status is UNCHECKABLE per the seam (`phase_not_in_roadmap`); it is not read as consistent.
  STATE.md and CONTEXT.md exist, so no axis was skipped. The success-criteria axis compared the roadmap goal and
  cross-cutting constraint, because the roadmap section has no "Success Criteria" list. One advisory finding
  (FD-1).
- **Not done by this pass.** No test, build, lint or docs command was run by the orchestrator. One reviewer
  (astra-review) reports a focused baseline run of 176 passing tests; that is the reviewer's statement and was not
  repeated.
